SimRubrics rubric authoring and the Wayfinder conversational agent gateway are optional extensions that use the same authenticated interfaces as human operators. Given a rubric and recorded encounters, OASIS produces per-criterion scores, evidence, and rationales, preserving execution artifacts for audit. Distinctive features include rubric-as-program compilation, progressive execution plans, content-addressable grading identity, transcript-augmented multimodal grading, explicit review state, and a shared command surface for humans and autonomous agents. Though developed in medical education, the architecture is domain-agnostic, applying wherever structured performance can be evaluated from recorded or written artifacts. In production at UT Southwestern Medical Center since Fall 2023, the platform has processed more than 7,000 encounters. This publication includes the report and project information, not application source, binaries, installation materials, sample data, or a tagged software release.

</details>


### [58] [Evaluating Enterprise Analytics Agents: An End-to-End, Trace-Backed Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09182)
*Teja Venkat Kolli,Sang Su Lee,Xueying Yan,Jessie Chen,Chi Cheng,Kartik Ravisankar,Shishir Dash,Vijay Anand Raghavan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出面向企业分析智能体的端到端评估方法，从语义理解、执行质量、可靠性三个维度对智能体行为打分，并通过运行轨迹而非仅最终答案来揭示失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析智能体不仅做文本转SQL，还需理解业务意图、选择指标定义与数据源、执行工具并生成影响决策的答案；仅评估最终答案会掩盖错误来源、遗漏必要拆解、无依据因果断言及跨运行不一致等问题。

Method: 方法包含三类评估族：语义理解、执行质量、可靠性；使用含人工金标准答案的题库、多次重复运行与运行时轨迹。每次运行先做运行有效性检查，再获得分层、支持弃答的评分，评分输入决策框架而非发布门禁。案例在大型在线市场内部受控分析智能体上实例化：50个分析问题、2个匿名模型配置、每配置3次随机重复，共300条轨迹。

Result: 高能力配置将早期拒答从73%降至0%，真实数据回答从21%升至73%；但16%运行耗尽工具轮次预算，77%轨迹超出模式探索预算，且50个问题中有41个改变了表解释。在带结构化金标准的财务问题上，源表使用与升级行为改善，但规范性拆解在两个配置中均较弱。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对分析智能体的信任需要端到端、轨迹支撑的评估，而不能仅依赖SQL正确性或最终答案质量。

Abstract: Enterprise analytics agents are not only text-to-SQL systems. They interpret business intent and choose metric definitions. They select data sources, execute tools, inspect results, and produce natural-language answers. Those answers may influence operational, financial, or executive decisions. Grading final answers hides where these agents fail. A plausible answer can use the wrong source of truth. It can skip a required decomposition, claim causality without support, or change its table interpretation across repeated runs. We present an end-to-end evaluation methodology for analytics agents. The methodology grades agent behavior across three families: semantic understanding, execution quality, and reliability. Grading uses question banks with human-written golden answers, repeated runs, and runtime traces. Each run first passes a run-validity check, then receives tiered, abstention-aware scores that feed a decision framework rather than a release gate. We instantiate the methodology on a controlled internal analytics agent at a large online marketplace. The case study uses 50 analytics questions, two anonymous model configurations, and three randomized repetitions per configuration, yielding 300 traces. The higher-capability configuration reduced early refusal from 73% to 0% and increased real-data answers from 21% to 73%. However, it also exhausted the tool-round budget on 16% of runs. It overran the schema-exploration budget on 77% of traces. It changed its table interpretation on 41 of 50 questions. On finance questions with structured golden answers, source table use and escalation improved, but canonical decomposition remained weak in both configurations. These results show why trust in analytics agents requires end-to-end, trace-backed evaluation rather than SQL correctness or final answer quality alone.

</details>


### [59] [The Double Measurement Confound in Agent Benchmarks: De-Scaffolding, Ground-Truth Scoring, and Reliability Beyond the Mean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09218)
*Yonghong Zhang,Shadi Motaali,Vu Phong Dinh,Avin Piroutiniya,Jorge E. López de Vergara,Luis de Pedro,Ricardo Correia,Isabel M. Parra,Yong Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文识别出LLM Agent基准测试中的双重测量混淆：执行关键决策由固定脚手架承担而非模型，评分器标准也可能偏离任务正确性。作者提出测量理论框架与审计-修复协议，在ComtradeBench上将近乎平坦的排行榜转化为可靠谱，并发现评分器效度是基准特定的、脚手架归属普遍不受控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent基准被广泛用于比较LLM并指导部署，但只有当分数反映模型能力而非评估流程属性时才有意义。现有评估存在双重测量混淆：执行关键决策由固定脚手架而非模型完成；评分器所用标准未必反映任务正确性。因此需要刻画"分数何时可解释为模型能力证据"的理论条件并加以修复。

Method: 提出统一的测量理论框架，刻画基准分数可解释为模型能力证据的条件；并实例化为审计-修复协议：(i) 将执行关键决策从脚手架转移给模型；(ii) 用带种子的真值评分替代基于形状的评估；(iii) 通过最坏情况与尾部风险指标报告均值之外的可靠性。在ComtradeBench上进行实验，并将审计应用于现有基准。

Result: 在ComtradeBench上，联合干预将原本近乎平坦的排行榜转化为可靠性谱，既能区分平均性能又能区分跨种子的鲁棒性。对现有基准的审计进一步显示：评分器效度是基准特定的，而脚手架归属在所有探测到的地方都是不受控的轴。

Conclusion: 基准分数应与其脚手架层级、评分标准和可靠性画像一并解读；该工作为更有效的LLM Agent评估提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks are increasingly used to compare large language models (LLMs) and guide deployment decisions, yet benchmark scores are meaningful only if they measure model capability rather than properties of the evaluation pipeline. We identify a double measurement confound: execution-critical decisions are performed by a fixed scaffold instead of the model, while the scorer evaluates outputs using criteria that may not reflect task correctness. We unify these issues within a measurement-theoretic framework that characterizes when benchmark scores can be interpreted as evidence of model capability, and instantiate it with an audit-and-repair protocol that (i) transfers execution-critical decisions from the scaffold to the model, (ii) replaces shape-based evaluation with seeded ground-truth scoring, and (iii) reports reliability beyond the mean through worst-case and tail-risk metrics. Experiments on ComtradeBench show that the joint intervention transforms a nearly flat leaderboard into a reliability spectrum that distinguishes both average performance and robustness across seeds. Applying the audit to existing benchmarks further shows that scorer validity is benchmark-specific, whereas scaffold ownership is an uncontrolled axis wherever we probed it. Our results suggest that benchmark scores should be interpreted together with their scaffolding level, scoring criterion, and reliability profile, providing a practical framework for more valid evaluation of LLM agents.

</details>


### [60] [Cost-Aware Post-Hoc Deferral Under Calibration and Shift: An Environmental AI Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09235)
*Haoran Yu,Lifei Liu,Danping Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出后验框架 EcoTrust，用六组错误风险估计器、类别非对称成本、审核者准确率与可选支持门控比较自动决策与人工复核，发现更丰富的风险信号未必优于校准后的置信度，且检测到外推并不意味着个案排序可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结分类器的延迟决策不能仅靠不确定度排序：置信度可能失校准、错误成本不对称、审核者会出错，且部署数据可能超出校准支持域。论文旨在刻画这些因素交互下的决策边界。

Method: EcoTrust 后验框架：采用六组错误风险估计器，纳入类别非对称成本、审核者准确率与可选支持门控（support gate），在哥伦比亚河热应激测试台上与 Chow 置信度规则对比；使用 12 个现成后端模型、成对年块自助法，并迁移至 10 个河流站点评估。

Result: 学习估计器将错误排序 AUC 从 0.869 提升到 0.889，但 Chow 规则分布内成本更低（0.416 对比 0.567/天）；12 个后端中，学习风险与校准匹配的类别感知置信度估计器各有 6 个优于原始 Chow，成对年块自助法无法区分二者平均成本差异；迁移至 10 站点时门控标记全部样本、退化为恒复核回退，仅在审核完美且无约束时于 8 个站点取得最低成本。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了单一受控任务上的决策边界：更丰富的风险信号不能可靠超越校准置信度，检测到外推也不意味着个案级排序可迁移。

Abstract: Choosing a deferral policy for a frozen classifier requires more than ranking uncertain cases: confidence may be miscalibrated, errors have unequal costs, reviewers can err, and deployment data can leave calibration support. We study these interactions through EcoTrust, a post-hoc framework that compares automatic action with review using a six-group error-risk estimator, class-asymmetric costs, reviewer accuracy, and an optional support gate. On a Columbia River thermal-stress testbed, the learned estimator improves error-ranking area under the receiver operating characteristic curve from 0.869 to 0.889, but Chow's confidence rule has lower in-distribution cost (0.416 versus 0.567 per day). Across 12 off-the-shelf backends, learned risk and a calibration-matched, class-aware confidence estimator each beat raw Chow on six; a paired year-block bootstrap does not resolve their mean cost difference. In transfer to ten river stations, the gate flags every case and becomes an always-review fallback, attaining the lowest cost on eight stations only when review is perfect and unconstrained. These results characterize decision boundaries on one controlled task: richer risk signals do not reliably improve on calibrated confidence, and detected extrapolation does not imply transferable case-level ranking.

</details>


### [61] [Talking to Itself While Coding: What Makes Comments Help Code Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09242)
*Dangfeng Pan,Zhensu Sun,Cenyuan Zhang,David Lo,Xiaoning Du*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 评论影响代码生成的并非其表面形式，而是其携带的"正确解题内容"：来自通过测试的源解答的评论平均提升17.2% pass@1，而失败解答的评论无可靠收益，跨题评论则下降20.8%；且这种增益难以通过提示复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在写代码时会生成自然语言评论，这些评论又成为后续代码生成的上下文，因此评论可能影响代码生成质量。但此前不清楚究竟是评论的哪些属性（频率、意图、表面形式还是所传达的解题内容）在起作用。作者希望厘清评论作用的来源，从而更好地理解并利用这一上下文机制。

Method: 研究结合观察性分析与受控干预。首先在LiveCodeBench上检验评论频率与宽泛的评论意图对pass@1的预测能力；随后采用预填充（prefill）范式，用较强源模型撰写的评论块去预填充较弱的接收模型，从而把评论的表面形式与其所传达的解题内容分离开来，并对比通过测试的源解答评论、失败解答评论以及跨题评论三种条件；最后在多种模型与提示变体下测试接收模型能否复现外部评论带来的增益。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上，评论频率和宽泛的评论意图都无法可靠预测pass@1。预填充实验中，来自通过测试的源解答的评论使接收模型pass@1平均提升17.2%；描述失败解答的评论未带来可靠增益；针对不同问题的评论则使pass@1下降20.8%。在广泛的模型与提示变体中，多数接收模型未能显著复现外部评论增益，最佳情况也仅恢复了24%。

Conclusion: 评论之所以有助于代码生成，并非因为"它是评论"这一形式属性，而是因为它能提供提示难以可靠引出的正确解题内容。这意味着评论的价值在于内容正确性而非表面形式，也说明仅靠提示工程难以替代评论所承载的正确解题信息。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often generate natural-language comments while writing code, and these comments become part of the context used to generate the code that follows. However, it remains unclear which properties of comments affect code-generation performance. We study this question through observational analyses and controlled interventions. On LiveCodeBench, neither comment frequency nor broad comment intent reliably predicts pass@1. We then prefill weaker recipient models with comment blocks written by stronger source models, allowing us to separate comment surface form from the solution content they convey. Comments from source solutions that pass the tests raise recipient pass@1 by 17.2% on average. In contrast, comments describing failed solutions provide no reliable gain, while comments written for a different problem reduce pass@1 by 20.8%. Finally, across a wide range of models and prompt variants, most recipient models show no significant recovery of the external-comment gain, and the best case recovers only 24%. These results show that comments help code generation not merely because they are comments, but because they can provide correct solution content that prompting cannot reliably elicit.

</details>


### [62] [How effective are traditional test criteria at detecting bugs in large language models generated code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09315)
*Asma Hamidi,Michael Konstantinou,Renzo Degiovanni,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过5个LLM与4个基准的端到端自动生成实验，收集6000+故障程序，评估语句覆盖、分支覆盖与变异测试在检出LLM引入故障上的有效性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试充分性准则此前主要基于人类编写的程序、故障与测试进行评估，而LLM代码生成的普及使这些准则能否有效检出LLM引入的故障成为未知问题，需系统性实证检验。

Method: 构建端到端工作流，由LLM同时自动生成代码与测试；涉及5个LLM、4个基准，收集6000+故障程序实例；对语句覆盖、分支覆盖、变异测试三种常用充分性准则的故障触发与检出效果及开销进行对比评估，并考察提示感知（prompt-aware）测试判定（oracle）的影响。

Result: 多数LLM引入的故障较易捕获；难以触发的故障对传统覆盖与变异准则均难覆盖；实际故障检出率极低甚至接近零，主因是测试判定无法识别由已生成测试前缀触发出的错误行为；提示感知判定可提升检出率但总体仍有限；变异测试在触发与检出上仅略优于传统覆盖准则。

Conclusion: 当前测试充分性准则在LLM生成代码场景下检出能力受限，核心瓶颈在于测试判定而非覆盖度；变异测试的边际收益难以justify其显著更高的应用成本；用户仍需人工推理测试断言，自动化测试生成存在关键局限。

Abstract: Test adequacy criteria are widely used to evaluate and guide software testing. Although prior research has extensively examined these criteria using human-written programs, faults, and tests, the increasing adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) for code generation raises important questions about their effectiveness in detecting LLM-induced faults. To investigate this, we conduct an empirical study involving 5 LLMs and 4 benchmarks, simulating end-to-end workflows in which both code and tests are automatically generated. We collect 6,000+ faulty program instances and evaluate the effectiveness and efficiency of 3 widely used adequacy criteria: statement coverage, branch coverage, and mutation testing. Our findings reveal several key insights. First, most faults introduced by LLMs are relatively trivial to catch. Second, the challenging faults are difficult to trigger using either traditional coverage-based or mutation-based criteria. Third, actual fault detection rates remain extremely low, often near zero, because test oracles fail to capture faulty behavior triggered by the generated test prefixes, exposing a critical limitation of automated test generation. Fourth, prompt-aware oracles can improve fault detection, but their overall effectiveness remains limited, highlighting the need for users to manually reason about test assertions. We further observe that mutation testing only marginally outperforms traditional coverage criteria in both triggering and detecting faults, raising questions about whether its significantly higher application cost is justified in this context.

</details>


### [63] [The Vibe Shift in Software Engineering: Evaluating AI-Led Conversational Programming for Performance, Cognition, and Responsible Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09560)
*Sales G. Aribe,Louie Jay S. Labastida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过混合方法实验评估"氛围编程"（Vibe Coding）这一AI主导的对话式编程范式，发现其显著提升开发效率但牺牲了软件质量，并提出负责任采用的三支柱框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型驱动的对话式编程范式兴起，开发者可通过自然语言交互生成软件，但其在性能效率、认知影响及负责任采用方面的综合影响尚缺乏系统评估，需要与传统编程及AI辅助编程进行对照研究。

Method: 采用混合方法设计，30名专业开发者与高级计算专业学生参与，在三种实验条件下完成等价编程任务。定量数据使用描述性统计与重复测量方差分析（repeated-measures ANOVA），定性数据采用主题分析（thematic analysis）。

Result: 氛围编程显著提升开发效率：任务完成时间较传统编程减少27%，较AI辅助编程减少12%。但可维护性指标下降、安全漏洞增加，存在质量权衡。可用性良好（SUS = 71.4），认知负荷中等（NASA-TLX = 55.5），体现句法负担降低但语言推理负担上升。主题分析识别出信任校准、控制丧失、认知适应与提示工程策略四个关键构念，其中感知的控制丧失因透明度与验证不足而与安全风险上升相关。

Conclusion: 氛围编程能提升生产力，但需批判性监督；据此提出负责任采用的三支柱框架：人机能力混合集成、人工监督与透明问责、情境感知部署，强调其作为变革性但过渡性的软件开发范式。

Abstract: This study evaluates Vibe Coding, an emerging AI-led conversational programming paradigm that enables developers to generate software through natural-language interaction with large language models. Using a mixed-methods design, the study assessed performance efficiency, cognitive implications, and responsible adoption in comparison with traditional and AI-assisted coding environments. Thirty participants, including professional developers and advanced computing students, completed equivalent programming tasks under three experimental conditions. Quantitative data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and repeated-measures ANOVA, while qualitative data were examined through thematic analysis. Results show that vibe coding significantly improved development efficiency, reducing task completion time by 27% compared with traditional coding and 12% compared with AI-assisted coding. However, these gains were accompanied by lower maintainability indices and higher security vulnerabilities, indicating trade-offs in software quality. Usability results yielded a good rating (SUS = 71.4), while cognitive workload remained moderate (NASA-TLX = 55.5), reflecting reduced syntactic effort but increased linguistic reasoning. Thematic analysis identified trust calibration, loss of control, cognitive adaptation, and prompt-engineering strategy as key constructs. Notably, perceived loss of control was associated with increased security risks due to reduced transparency and validation of AI-generated outputs. Based on these findings, the study proposes a three-pillar framework for responsible adoption: hybrid integration of human and AI capabilities, human oversight and transparent accountability, and context-aware deployment. Overall, vibe coding enhances productivity but requires critical oversight, reinforcing its role as a transformative yet transitional paradigm in software development.

</details>


### [64] [Introducing Consort: A Spec-First Agent Framework for Enforced, Test-Driven Development on Live Database Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09671)
*Kevin Hartman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文将"规范先行、智能体驱动"的开发框架按工程纪律的强制方式分为三类：说服式、前置结构式、以及智能体无法编辑的硬控制式，并提出 Consort——一个基于第三类模式的规范先行、测试驱动框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当智能体编写代码时，开发框架成为非确定性工作者的控制系统。2025 年以来规范先行框架迅速普及，且它们在"预先捕获意图"上已达成共识，因此差异不在于是否捕获意图，而在于各自如何强制维持代码整洁、正确与可维护的工程纪律。

Method: 提出三种强制模式分类：（1）说服式强制——依赖可能被模型忽略的提示纪律；（2）前置结构式强制——强规范加可信构建；（3）不可编辑控制式强制——确定性编排器、人工审批门、不可变测试，以及必须针对实时分支数据库通过的绿色结果。据此构建 Consort：由确定性编排器驱动多个独立角色智能体，在实时数据库分支上依次经过规范先行设计通道与测试驱动构建通道。

Result: 论文以规范性论点形式给出主张：将测试与门禁以代码形式强制，可保持智能体所写代码的诚实性与可验证性；其专门化角色（如同此前的人类角色）是代码可维护性的来源。上述主张被表述为一项预注册、可检验的假设，尚未给出实证结果。

Conclusion: 框架间的关键区分在于工程纪律的强制机制；Consort 采用智能体运行其中但无法绕过的硬控制路线，作者据此提出关于可验证性与可维护性的预注册可检验假设，供后续实证验证。

Abstract: When an agent writes code, the development framework becomes the control system for a non-deterministic worker. Spec-first, agent-driven frameworks have gained rapid traction since 2025; the installable ones, GitHub Spec Kit, obra/superpowers, BMAD, and GSD, and our own, all capture intent through a specification or durable planning artifacts. Since they agree on capturing intent up front, what separates them is how each enforces the engineering discipline that keeps agent-written code clean, correct, and maintainable. Every framework enforces that discipline somehow; they differ in how. We characterize three modes: enforcement by persuasion (prompt discipline the model may ignore), by front-loaded structure (strong specs, then a trusted build), and through controls the agent cannot edit (a deterministic orchestrator, human-approved gates, immutable tests, and a green result that must pass against a live, branched database). We introduce Consort, a spec-first, test-driven agent framework built on the third, enforcing that discipline through controls the agent runs inside but cannot bypass, in which a deterministic orchestrator drives separate role agents through a spec-first design lane and a test-driven build lane on a live database branch. We argue that enforcing the tests and gates in code keeps agent-written code honest and verifiable, while its specialized roles, like the human roles before them, are what make it maintainable, claims we frame as a pre-registered, testable hypothesis.

</details>


### [65] [XAgent: eXecution-guided Agentic AI for Effective Localization and Resolution of GitHub Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09769)
*Hieu Huynh,Patanamon Thongtanunam,Michael Fu,Bach Le,Kla Tantithamthavorn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 XAgent，一个执行引导的智能体框架，通过分析动态行为与额外程序上下文来定位和验证仓库级 GitHub issue；在 SWE-bench-lite 上达到 62.0% 解决率与 72.8% 函数定位准确率，并保持成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的智能体方法依赖有限的 issue 静态描述，导致定位错误与验证不完整；仅凭描述会使 LLM 推理局限于 issue 描述的狭窄范围，生成无法解决根本问题的残缺补丁。

Method: 提出 XAgent，一个执行引导（execution-guided）的智能体框架，利用动态行为分析和额外程序上下文来定位 issue 并进行验证。

Result: 在 SWE-bench-lite 数据集上，XAgent 优于现有方法，解决率达 62.0%，函数定位准确率达 72.8%，同时保持成本效率；额外成功解决了 7 个顶级基线无法处理的 issue。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着从静态、面向描述的补丁生成，转向动态执行引导的 issue 解决，为基于 LLM 的编码智能体实现更鲁棒、更可泛化的软件维护开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Agentic AI has enabled capabilities in leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to autonomously resolve repository-level GitHub issues. However, due to the reliance on limited static description of issues, existing agentic approaches suffer from incorrect localization and incomplete validation. Solely relying on this information can bias LLM reasoning toward the narrow scope of the issue description, leading to incomplete patches that fail to address the underlying issue. In this paper, we present XAgent, an execution-guided agentic framework that analyzes dynamic behavior and additional program context to localize and validate issues. The experimental results on the SWE-bench-lite dataset demonstrate that XAgent outperforms other existing approaches, achieving a resolve rate of 62.0% and a function localization accuracy of 72.8%, while maintaining cost efficiency. Our analysis further shows that XAgent successfully resolves 7 additional issues that the top existing baselines fail to address. This work highlights a shift from static, description-oriented patch generation toward dynamic execution-guided issue resolution, opening new opportunities for LLM-based coding agents to achieve more robust and generalizable software maintenance.

</details>


### [66] [Keep Evaluation Fair: Detecting Data Leakage in Code Generation Benchmarks via Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09865)
*Dongdong Zhao,Jian Chen,Guancheng Lin,Jianwen Xiang,Jacky Wai Keung,Xiao Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CGMIA方法，通过影子模型+多源特征（专家特征与CodeBERT语义特征）的集成学习，检测代码生成基准的成员泄露，在8个基准上多数优于8种基线，并能识别StarCoder-7B中已知泄露的APPS样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成基准的数据泄露会虚高LLM评测性能、破坏评测有效性。现有DetectLeak依赖困惑度，仅反映对代码模式的通用熟悉度，在复杂或稀有样本上表现差，且忽略代码相似度、功能正确性、语义表示等有用信号。

Method: CGMIA（面向代码生成的成员推断攻击）：在基准样本子集上微调影子模型，构造带标签的成员/非成员数据；对每个样本采集输入提示、生成代码与参考解，提取专家特征（CodeBLEU、编辑距离、测试通过率、困惑度）及CodeBERT嵌入的语义特征；用集成学习模块融合特征，兼顾表层记忆信号与深层行为模式，训练分类器预测样本是否在目标模型训练集中。

Result: 在8个代码生成基准上，CGMIA在多数情况下优于8种现有成员推断方法；并有效检测出StarCoder-7B训练数据中已知泄露的APPS样本。

Conclusion: 融合专家特征与语义特征的成员推断方法可提升代码生成基准泄露检测的准确性与适用性，为更可靠的LLM代码能力评测提供支持。

Abstract: Code generation benchmarks are widely used to evaluate Large Language Models (LLMs), but benchmark data leakage into training sets can inflate performance and undermine evaluation validity. DetectLeak, a method specifically designed for code generation benchmark leakage detection, relies on perplexity scores to identify likely leaked samples. However, perplexity mainly reflects general familiarity with code patterns and may perform poorly on complex or rare samples. It also overlooks other useful signals, such as code similarity, functional correctness, and semantic representations. To address these limitations, we propose CGMIA (Code-Generation-specific Membership Inference Attack), a method for detecting leakage in code generation benchmarks. CGMIA fine-tunes a shadow model on a subset of benchmark samples to construct labeled member and non-member data. For each sample, it collects the input prompt, generated code, and reference solution, and extracts expert features, including CodeBLEU, edit distance, test pass rate, and perplexity, together with semantic features from CodeBERT embeddings. An integrated learning module combines these features to capture both surface-level memorization signals and deeper behavioral patterns, enabling a classifier to predict whether a sample was included in the target model's training set. Experiments on eight code generation benchmarks show that CGMIA outperforms eight existing membership inference methods in most cases. It also effectively detects known leaked APPS samples in StarCoder-7B's training data.

</details>


### [67] [Socio-technical and Ethical Dimensions of Architecture Practices in FLOSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09975)
*Sven Thielen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨FLOSS中软件架构实践如何受社会技术与伦理因素影响，并研究教育如何支持更显性、包容、反思性的架构工作，采用三阶段设计并产出实证证据、概念框架与轻量实践及教学形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLOSS在数字主权中具有重要作用，但其架构决策往往缺乏文档记录，散落于issue、pull request和邮件列表中；既有研究虽已涉及架构制品、架构侵蚀与沟通，但架构工作、治理安排与伦理承诺之间的相互作用仍研究不足。

Method: 采用三阶段研究设计：(1) 对3-4组领域配对的、架构上非平凡的FLOSS项目开展多方法案例研究；(2) 与实践者和教育者共同设计框架与干预方案；(3) 在项目与课程中进行试点评估。

Result: 预期产出三项成果：(i) 关于FLOSS架构实践的跨案例实证证据；(ii) 将架构实践与社会技术条件和伦理维度相连接的概念框架；(iii) 使架构工作更显性、更包容的轻量级实践与教学形式。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在弥合FLOSS架构实践中社会技术、治理与伦理因素研究的空白，并通过框架、实践与教育干预，推动更显性、包容且具反思性的架构工作。

Abstract: This project investigates how software architecture practices in Free/Libre Open Source Software (FLOSS) are shaped by socio-technical and ethical factors, and how education can support more explicit, inclusive, and reflective architectural work. Motivated by FLOSS's role in digital sovereignty, it is observed that architectural decisions are often undocumented and scattered across issues, pull requests, and mailing lists. While prior research has studied architectural artifacts, erosion, and communication, the interplay between architectural work, governance arrangements, and ethical commitments in FLOSS remains underexplored. The research follows a three-phase design: (1) multi-method case studies of 3-4 domain-pairs of architecturally non-trivial FLOSS projects, (2) framework and intervention design with practitioners and educators, and (3) pilot evaluations in projects and courses. It will produce (i) cross-case empirical evidence on FLOSS architecture practices, (ii) a conceptual framework linking architecture practices to socio-technical conditions and ethical dimensions, and (iii) lightweight practices and teaching formats that render architectural work more explicit and inclusive.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Repository Boundaries: Cross-Repository Graph Retrieval for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09987)
*Minh Le-Anh,Nam Le Hai,Quyen Tran,Anh Nguyen Hoang,Linh Ngo Van,Bach Le,Nghi D. Q. Bui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出跨仓库代码生成框架CrossCoder，通过统一知识图谱整合外部库，并结合规划、语义检索与多跳邻域扩展获取上下文，同时构建VersionExec基准评估依赖版本兼容性，实验显示功能正确性与版本鲁棒性均提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索式仓库级代码生成方法主要从本地仓库检索上下文，外部API使用依赖模型预训练知识，对未见或特定版本API不足；且检索策略多聚焦一跳证据，忽略代码组件间的结构关系。

Method: CrossCoder构建覆盖仓库与库实体的统一知识图谱，显式将外部库纳入检索上下文；通过规划与语义检索识别重要节点，再选择性扩展邻居节点，获得多跳上下文证据用于生成。同时提出基于BigCodeBench的VersionExec执行型基准，评估不同依赖版本下的生成表现。

Result: 在RepoExec、DevEval和VersionExec上，CrossCoder持续提升功能正确性（pass@1最高提升6.3%）以及对依赖版本变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 跨仓库知识图谱与多跳检索可有效融合外部库依赖信息，缓解版本特定API知识不足问题，为依赖版本兼容的仓库级代码生成提供了有效方案与评测基准。

Abstract: Repository-level code generation requires generated code to be compatible not only with the target repository but also with its dependency environment. Existing retrieval-based methods mainly retrieve context from the local repository, leaving external API usage dependent on the model's pretrained knowledge, which can be insufficient for unseen or version-specific APIs. Moreover, current retrieval strategies largely focus on one-hop evidence and overlook the structural relationships among code components. We propose CrossCoder, a cross-repository code generation framework that explicitly incorporates external libraries into the retrieval context through a unified knowledge graph over repository and library entities. CrossCoder identifies important nodes via planning and semantic retrieval, then selectively expands neighboring nodes to retrieve richer multi-hop contextual evidence for generation. To further evaluate dependency-version compatibility, we introduce VersionExec, an execution-based benchmark derived from BigCodeBench that evaluates generation under different dependency versions. Experimental results on RepoExec, DevEval, and VersionExec demonstrate that CrossCoder consistently improves both functional correctness (up to 6.3% on pass@1) and robustness to dependency-version changes.

</details>


### [69] [GraphDroid: Asynchronous LLM-Based Mobile App GUI Testing via History-Aware Exploration and Hybrid Intent Fulfillment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10031)
*Xiaolei Li,Jialun Cao,Zhijian Hou,Yuzhi Zhao,Yepang Liu,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 GraphDroid——一种意图驱动的 GUI 测试框架，通过聚类记忆、异步意图生成与混合意图履行策略，在 41 款 Android 应用上实现最高 36.4% 的覆盖率提升，成本不足最佳纯 LLM 基线的 1/8，并发现 19 个缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化 GUI 测试难以覆盖需要多步动作序列的复杂功能。传统工具缺乏语义理解，难以合成此类序列；现有 LLM 方案存在三点不足：(1) 无法利用历史上下文识别未覆盖功能；(2) 同步式意图生成阻塞探索；(3) 逐步调用 LLM 履行意图带来高成本与高延迟。

Method: GraphDroid 是意图驱动的 GUI 测试框架，包含三项关键设计：基于聚类的记忆机制，从历史访问状态中识别未覆盖功能；异步意图生成范式，消除延迟瓶颈；混合意图履行策略，将复杂意图交由 LLM 处理，简单意图下放给轻量启发式算法。

Result: 在 41 个真实 Android 应用上与 6 个 SOTA 基线对比：代码覆盖率最高提升 36.4%，成本低于最佳纯 LLM 基线的 1/8；在 41 个应用中暴露 19 个缺陷，在 Themis 缺陷基准的 52 个崩溃中检测出 13 个，均超越全部基线。19 个缺陷中 7 个为先前未知，已提交开发者，其中 4 个已确认并修复。

Conclusion: GraphDroid 通过记忆机制、异步生成与混合履行的协同设计，在覆盖率、成本与缺陷检测上全面优于现有方案，验证了意图驱动测试结合 LLM 与启发式方法的有效性。

Abstract: Automated GUI testing is a widely adopted technique for ensuring mobile application quality by simulating user interactions to exercise functionalities. Despite the research breakthroughs in the past decades, covering complex functionalities that require multi-step action sequences still remains challenging. Traditional tools lack semantic understanding capability and can rarely synthesize such action sequences. Recent LLM-based tools can generate test intents describing target functionalities and leverage the LLM to fulfill the intents, but suffer from three key limitations: 1) loss of historical context for identifying uncovered functionalities, 2) synchronous intent generation that blocks exploration, and 3) per-step LLM-driven fulfillment incurring high cost and latency. To address these limitations, we propose GraphDroid, an intent-driven GUI testing framework that integrates a cluster-based memory mechanism to effectively identify uncovered functionalities from historically visited states for comprehensive application testing. For improving testing efficiency, GraphDroid adopts an asynchronous intent generation paradigm that eliminates the latency bottleneck and a hybrid intent fulfillment strategy that reserves the LLM for fulfilling complex intents while delegating simple intents to a lightweight heuristic algorithm. We evaluate GraphDroid on 41 real-world Android apps against six state-of-the-art baselines. Results show that GraphDroid outperforms all baselines, achieving up to 36.4% higher code coverage while incurring less than one eighth of the cost of the best pure LLM-based baseline. GraphDroid also exposes 19 bugs in the 41 apps and detects 13 of 52 crashes in the Themis bug benchmark, surpassing all the six baselines. Seven of the 19 bugs were previously unknown and we reported them to the developers. So far, four bugs have been confirmed and fixed.

</details>


### [70] [If It's Not Buggy, Don't Fix It: On the Dynamics of Iterative Bug-fixing with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10123)
*Xietao Wang-Lin,Anton Isopoussu,Louis Mahon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文揭示LLM作为自动化修复器在迭代盲用下的“伪修复”现象：模型会对完全无bug的程序误报并“修复”，其破坏正确代码的速率甚至高于修复有bug代码的速率，并会陷入反复增删同一改动的死循环；机制探测发现存在控制“编辑倾向”的引导向量，对应模型内部的“bug代码”表征被错误激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛用于软件开发，基于LLM的自动化程序修复工具越来越多地被用于代码审查场景。然而在缺乏明确停止条件、对模型输出不加甄别的“迭代盲用”情况下，其行为是否可靠尚不清楚，因此需要系统研究全自主bug修复系统的动态特性与失效模式。

Method: 1) 在多种模型与多种修复环境下开展“迭代盲用”实验，让LLM反复充当bug修复器；2) 对比其在不同程序上的行为：对完全无bug程序的“检出/修复”率，与对有bug程序的真实修复率；3) 考察该迭代过程的长期动态，观察是否出现周期性行为；4) 通过机制可解释性探测（mechanistic probing），寻找并验证控制“编辑倾向”的引导向量（steering vector）。

Result: 跨多个模型和修复环境一致发现：LLM会对完全无bug的程序宣称检测到bug，而无bug程序的“破坏”率高于有bug程序的成功修复率；长期迭代中，过程频繁进入“伪bug修复”循环，即同一改动被反复加入又删除、无限循环；机制探测证实存在一个可控制编辑倾向的引导向量，说明模型内部存在“bug代码”的内部表征，而该表征被错误激活是导致伪修复的原因。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了完全自主bug修复系统的动态规律，指出在目标模糊（是否还有bug不明确）时，LLM的迭代修复并不收敛于真正的修复，反而可能持续劣化代码。这为设计自主修复系统的停止条件、避免伪修复循环提供了依据，也表明需对LLM内部“buggy code”表征进行干预以抑制错误的编辑倾向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become ubiquitous in software development, with LLM-based automated program repair tools increasingly used during code review. In this report, we explore the iterative blind use of LLMs as bug-fixers. Across multiple models and repair environments, we find that LLMs consistently claim to detect bugs in entirely bug-free programs while the rate of repair of buggy programs is less than that of the damage to correct programs. We also explore the long-term dynamics of this iterative process, and find that this frequently reaches a pseudo-bug-fixing cycle where the same changes are added and removed again ad infinitum. Lastly, via mechanistic probing, we unveil the existence of a steering vector which controls the editing propensity, suggesting that LLMs have an internal representation of ``buggy code", and that this representation is what is falsely activated to induce pseudo-bug fixing. These results provide insight towards the dynamics of fully autonomous bug-fixing systems, as well as stopping conditions under ambiguous goals.

</details>


### [71] [A-JIT: Agentic Just-In-Time Software Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10248)
*Mark Marron,Earl T. Barr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出A-JIT范式，用可持续演化的动态软件系统取代静态二进制制品，将代码、运行时框架与内嵌AI智能体整合为应用整体，类比JIT编译器按执行路径特化机器码，使软件逻辑、工作流与工具接口在运行时按用户需求特化与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件交付基于静态范式：代码在执行前构造并作为固定制品部署，难以应对需求与用户行为的持续变化，缺乏对运行时使用情况的感知与自适应能力。

Method: 提出A-JIT（Agentic Just-In-Time Software Construction）范式。应用由代码、运行时harness与内嵌AI智能体三部分集成；该智能体持续观察系统使用与实时执行轨迹，借鉴传统JIT编译器将机器码特化到运行时执行路径的思想，对软件逻辑、工作流和工具接口进行运行时特化，在应用程序生命周期中直接集成综合（synthesis）过程，动态构造缺失实现并按需生成新能力。

Result: 该模型可支持轨迹驱动的人机协同构建（trace-driven human-AI co-construction），并开辟面向自适应、自演化软件的新设计空间，使应用能动态构造缺失实现、即时生成新功能并持续适应用户行为。

Conclusion: A-JIT将JIT式特化思想从机器码层面提升到软件逻辑与工作流层面，为从静态交付转向持续演化的动态软件系统提供了新范式与设计空间。

Abstract: Traditional software delivery assumes a static paradigm: code is constructed prior to execution and deployed as a fixed artifact. We present Agentic Just-In-Time Software Construction (A-JIT), a paradigm that replaces static binaries with dynamic, software systems that can perpetually evolve to meet changing demands. In A-JIT, an application is an integrated assembly comprising code, a runtime harness, and an embedded AI agent that continuously observes system usage and live execution traces. Much like a traditional JIT compiler specializes machine code to runtime execution paths, A-JIT specializes software logic, workflows, and tool interfaces to meet the specific needs of the end-user. By integrating synthesis directly into the ambient application lifecycle, A-JIT enables applications to dynamically construct missing implementations, generate new capabilities on the fly, and continuously adapt to end-user behavior. We demonstrate how this model supports trace-driven human-AI co-construction and opens a new design space for adaptive, self-evolving software.

</details>


### [72] [Ensembling LLMs for AI-Augmented Cybersecurity Software Requirements Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10316)
*Santiago Perez-Acuna,Yod-Samuel Martín,Juan C. Yelmo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文将LLM在安全需求生成中的运行间差异视为资源，提出后生成集成（Uniform与Naive-Bayes融合）以聚合多次随机输出，从而兼顾覆盖度与排序质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从安全标准到系统特定需求的转化是网络安全需求工程的核心，但工作量大且对召回敏感。LLM虽可加速该过程，单次运行却不可靠：既漏掉有效防护措施，又产生貌似合理的幻觉，且输出在不同运行与模型间波动。作者不试图挑选单一最优输出，而是把这些波动性重新理解为可利用的资源。

Method: 采用后生成集成（post-generation ensembling）：用信息检索中的数据融合算子聚合多次随机运行的输出。提出两种策略——Uniform融合（奖励跨运行一致性）与Naive-Bayes融合（按每次运行的估计可靠性加权）。实验覆盖4个模型族的12种配置、共24次运行，针对10条ISO/IEC 27002:2022控制项生成需求，并由专家对照72条有效需求的黄金标准进行评判。采用PR曲线、ROC曲线下面积及内部验证（out-of-bag bootstrap重采样、结构化扰动样本）评估稳定性。

Result: 汇总所有运行输出可召回全部72条有效需求（单一配置平均不足一半），但同时引入111条幻觉。融合能有效区分有效需求与幻觉，将有效需求排在幻觉之前。Uniform融合的PR-AUC与ROC-AUC分别比最佳单一配置高0.142和0.118；Naive-Bayes加权再提升0.039和0.052，达到0.864和0.869，并更早获得可用操作点。增益稳定：在至少92%的out-of-bag bootstrap重采样及全部结构化扰动样本中保持正向。

Conclusion: 后生成融合把表面噪声转化为实际资产：仅使用低成本的非前沿模型，即可通过一个轻量层为分析师提供更广的覆盖范围与更优的优先审查队列。

Abstract: Translating high-level controls from security standards into concrete, system-specific requirements is central to cybersecurity requirements engineering. Large language models (LLMs) can accelerate this labor-intensive, recall-sensitive task, but any single run is unreliable: it misses valid safeguards while introducing plausible hallucinations, and outputs shift across runs and models. We reframe this variability as a resource: rather than selecting one output, we study post-generation ensembling, aggregating stochastic runs with information-retrieval data-fusion operators. We propose two strategies: Uniform fusion rewards mere cross-run agreement, whereas Naive-Bayes fusion weights each run by its estimated reliability. We evaluate both over 24 runs from 12 configurations across four model families, generated for ten ISO/IEC 27002:2022 controls and expert-judged against a gold standard of 72 valid requirements. Pooling every run's output recovers all 72 (whereas single configurations recover on average under half) but also 111 hallucinations. Fusion separates the wheat from the chaff, ranking valid requirements well ahead of hallucinations. In the areas under the precision-recall and ROC curves, Uniform fusion alone largely surpasses every original run and configuration by 0.142 and 0.118 over the best configuration. Naive-Bayes weighting adds a further 0.039 and 0.052, reaching 0.864 and 0.869 while attaining useful operating points earlier. Internal validation confirms the stability of these gains: they stay positive in at least 92% of out-of-bag bootstrap resamples and every structured-perturbation sample. Post-generation fusion thus turns apparent noise into a practical asset: a lightweight layer giving analysts broader coverage and a better prioritized review queue, using affordable, below-frontier models alone.

</details>


### [73] [Retrofitting Code Using LLMs to Support Exceptional Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10397)
*Linghan Zhong,Jiyang Zhang,Jayanth Srinivasa,Junyi Jessy Li,Milos Gligoric*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出新任务"异常相关代码回填"(ERC Retrofitting)：给定无ERC的代码与异常行为测试，自动生成缺失的异常相关代码使测试通过；设计EXCODER结合静态/动态程序分析与LLM进行上下文工程，在304个方法基准上Qwen 2.5 Coder 32b达pass@1 85.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常相关代码（ERC，包括throw语句、守卫throw的if条件及try/catch块）是软件系统的重要组成部分，用于检测和处理偏离预期行为的异常状态。但大型代码库中手工编写ERC繁琐耗时，因此需要自动化手段。

Method: 定义新任务：给定不含ERC的代码及异常行为测试（EBT，如检查参数为空时方法是否抛出InvalidArgumentException），自动生成缺失的ERC使测试通过。设计并实现EXCODER，通过上下文工程（context engineering）将静态程序分析与动态程序分析提取的上下文信息提供给LLM，辅助其完成该任务。构建基准：从GitHub Java仓库的75个项目、304个方法中系统性地移除ERC。

Result: 结合Qwen 2.5 Coder 32b，在开发者编写的测试套件上，EXCODER的pass@1、pass@5、pass@10分别为85.92%（较基线高12.56个百分点）、86.18%（高12.82 p.p.）、86.51%（高13.15 p.p.）。

Conclusion: EXCODER为该问题提供了有效但不完美的自动化代码生成方案，首次让开发者能够以测试驱动开发的方式实现ERC；对生成代码的人工检查揭示了EXCODER的局限性，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Exception Related Code (ERC), which includes throw statements, conditions (if statements) that guard those throw statements, and try/catch blocks, is an essential component of software systems, allowing developers to detect and handle exceptional states that deviate from the expected program behavior. However, manually writing ERC across large codebases is tedious. We propose a novel task: retrofitting existing code with ERC. Namely, given code (without ERC) and Exceptional Behavior Tests (EBTs) (e.g., check if method throws InvalidArgumentException if null is given as the value to the argument) we aim to automatically generate missing ERC, such that the given tests pass. We design and implement Exception Coder (EXCODER) that performs context engineering to help Large Language Models (LLMs) tackle this task. EXCODER integrates static and dynamic program analysis with LLMs by providing the extracted contextual information to the LLMs. To evaluate EXCODER, we build a benchmark constructed from GitHub Java repositories, where we systematically remove ERC in 304 methods from 75 projects. Our results demonstrate that EXCODER provides an effective, though imperfect, solution to this problem in automated code generation, offering developers the first way to implement ERC following test-driven development. When combined with Qwen 2.5 Coder 32b, EXCODER achieves pass@1, 5, and 10 rates of 85.92% (12.56 percentage points over baseline), 86.18% (12.82 p.p. over baseline), and 86.51% (13.15 p.p. over baseline), respectively, on developer-written test suites. Our manual inspection of the generated code further reveals limitations of EXCODER, pointing to directions for future work.

</details>


### [74] [Towards Scalable and Cost-Efficient Vulnerability Detection: A Study on Automatic Query Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10412)
*Ivana Clairine Irsan,Ratnadira Widyasari,Huihui Huang,Ting Zhang,Yue Liu,Ouh Eng Lieh,Shar Lwin Khin,Kang Hong Jin,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实证评估LLM基于NVD漏洞数据合成CodeQL查询的能力，发现LLM生成的查询显著提升基线CodeQL查询，平均F1-score提高82%，并证明该方式比直接LLM扫描整个代码库更具可扩展性与成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析（如CodeQL）是软件安全的基石，但其高覆盖率查询套件需大量人工开发。LLM虽被视为自动化代码推理的潜在方案，但在生成结构化、可执行安全查询方面的实用价值尚未被充分探索，因此需系统评估LLM自动生成CodeQL查询的可行性与效果。

Method: 开展实证研究，利用国家漏洞数据库（NVD）的漏洞数据，评估LLM合成CodeQL查询的能力；在多样化的真实漏洞集合上，系统评测多种LLM架构生成查询的表现，并以提升检测覆盖率与精度为指标进行衡量，同时进行成本效益分析。

Result: LLM生成的查询显著增强基线CodeQL查询，平均F1-score提升82%；成本效益分析表明，直接使用LLM扫描整个代码库在计算与经济上常不可行，而用LLM合成CodeQL查询则提供了可扩展、成本更低的替代方案。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效弥合非结构化漏洞报告与形式化静态分析规范之间的差距，为全面自动化漏洞检测提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: Static analysis remains a cornerstone of software security, yet the effectiveness of tools such as CodeQL is often limited by the substantial manual effort required to develop high-coverage query suites. While large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a potential solution for automated code reasoning, their practical utility in generating structured, executable security queries remains underexplored. In this paper, we conduct an empirical study to evaluate the ability of LLMs to synthesize CodeQL queries using vulnerability data from the National Vulnerability Database. Through this investigation, we explore the potential of using LLMs as an automatic CodeQL query generator. Subsequently, we systematically evaluate the performance of various LLM architectures across a diverse set of real-world vulnerabilities, measuring their ability to improve detection coverage and precision. Our findings reveal that LLM-generated queries significantly enhance the baseline CodeQL queries, yielding 82% improvement in average F1-score. Furthermore, we provide a detailed cost- benefit analysis showing that while direct LLM-based scanning of entire repositories is often computationally and financially prohibitive, leveraging LLMs to synthesize CodeQL queries offers a scalable and cost-effective alternative for large-scale vulnerability detection. Our results suggest that LLMs can effectively bridge the gap between unstructured vulnerability reports and formal static analysis specifications, offering a scalable path toward comprehensive automated vulnerability detection.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [A practical DIRECT-type algorithm for medium-scale black-box global optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09796)
*Linas Stripinis,Remigijus Paulavičius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出X-DTC-GL，一种结合动态划分与混合策略的DIRECT型全局优化算法，在四个基准测试集上显著优于现有DIRECT基线，解的可求解性提升约12%，解质量提升约27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DIRECT算法虽具有确定性与均衡的探索-利用策略，但主要适用于低维问题，随维度增加收敛变慢，限制了其在复杂优化任务中的适用性。

Method: 提出X-DTC-GL算法，包含两部分：动态划分策略，基于局部一维代理模型自适应细化搜索空间，快速细分有潜力的超矩形；混合策略，选择性使用爬山法对代理模型识别出的有潜力区域进行开发。

Result: 在四个不同基准测试集上的大量实验表明，X-DTC-GL显著优于现有DIRECT型基线：可求解性提升约12%，解质量提升约27%；性能剖析分析显示其在多达约40%的实例上收敛最快，在约17%的问题上运行时间最优，整体执行时间具竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过在基于划分的框架内改进性能，X-DTC-GL增强了算法在最先进黑盒优化中的竞争力。

Abstract: The DIRECT algorithm is a deterministic global optimization method known for its versatility and balanced exploration-exploitation strategy. However, DIRECT-type algorithms are primarily effective for low-dimensional problems and often exhibit slow convergence as dimensionality increases, limiting their applicability to more complex optimization tasks. To address this limitation, this paper introduces X-DTC-GL, a novel DIRECT-type algorithm that incorporates dynamic partitioning and hybridization techniques. The dynamic partitioning approach adaptively refines the search space based on local one-dimensional surrogate models, enabling rapid subdivision of promising hyper-rectangles. The hybridization strategy selectively employs a hill-climbing method to exploit promising regions identified by the surrogate models. Extensive experiments on four diverse benchmark suites demonstrate that X-DTC-GL significantly outperforms existing DIRECT-type baselines, achieving improvements of ~12% in solvability and ~27% in solution quality. Performance-profile analyses indicate the fastest convergence on up to ~40% of instances, the best runtime performance on ~17% of problems, and competitive overall execution times. By improving performance within the partition-based framework, these advances strengthen the algorithm's competitiveness in state-of-the-art black-box optimization.

</details>


### [76] [Spectral origin of the topological gap exponent d + η: mechanism, kernel, decomposition, and scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09159)
*Matthew Loftus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从解析与数值两方面论证临界点云拓扑间隙 Δ 的标度律 Δ~L^{d+η}，其来源是红外主导的谱积分 I(-2η) ~ L^{d+η}，并分解为体积因子 I_0 与形状因子 I_shape；物理上仅 d=2 满足红外主导条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑间隙 Δ 定义为临界点云的 H_1 总持续度相对于密度匹配零模型的超出量，其经验标度 Δ~L^{d+η} 缺乏解析解释。作者希望从谱积分出发给出该标度律的推导，厘清体积效应与反常维度（anomalous dimension）各自的贡献，并明确该机制成立的维度条件，从而解释为何 d=3 中必须做密度归一化。

Method: 1) 解析推导：考虑谱积分 I(α)=Σ_{k≠0} S_conn(k)|k|^α，在红外主导下证明 I(α)~L^{2-α-η}，取 α=-2η 得 I(-2η)~L^{d+η}；将 I(-2η) 分解为 I_0·I_shape，其中 I_0∝N(1-m²)~L^d 为体积因子，I_shape~L^η 为反常维度。2) 维度判据：证明机制要求 d<2+η（红外主导），故物理体系限于 d=2；d=3 时谱积分紫外主导。3) 数值验证：对 Potts q=4 做 α-扫描（L=32–256），拟合最优 α；考察 ⟨m²·I(-2η)⟩ 超标度积及其协方差；在 Divol–Polonik 启发式框架下分析裸 PH 核与有效核的 k-依赖性；用偏相关检验 Δ 与 I(-0.5) 的逐构型一致性。

Result: 解析上得到 Δ~L^{d+η} 与 I(-2η)=I_0·I_shape 的分解，并证明红外主导条件 d<2+η。数值上 Potts q=4 的 α_opt 落在 [-0.75,-0.5]，与 -2η_Ising 一致、与 -2η_{q=4}=-1 不符（作者标注为待 L≥1024 确认的暂定结论）。⟨m²·I(-2η)⟩ 超标度积由相关 r(m²,I)≈-0.98 主导（源于共享的 I_0 振幅），故按协方差修正处理。启发式分析显示裸 PH 核平坦，有效核仅在临界性时获得 k-依赖。逐构型上 Δ 与 I(-0.5) 的一致性主要是磁化相关：L=256 时 R²=0.91，剔除 |M| 偏相关后降至 R²≈0，支持 I_0 的 Parseval 恒等式但不支持 |k|^{-2η} 形状因子，后者依赖系综 L-标度确立。

Conclusion: 拓扑间隙的标度律可由红外主导谱积分解析给出，体积因子 I_0 与形状因子 I_shape 分离，机制在物理上仅适用于 d=2。数值结果（α_opt∈[-0.75,-0.5]、R² 塌缩）初步支持该图景，但 |k|^{-2η} 形状因子仍需更大尺度系综标度进一步确认，α_opt 的 Potts q=4 结论为暂定性。

Abstract: The topological gap $Δ$ -- the excess $H_1$ total persistence of a critical point cloud over a density-matched null -- scales as $Δ\sim L^{d+η}$. We derive this analytically: the spectral integral $I(α) = \sum_{k\neq 0} S_{\mathrm{conn}}(k)\,|k|^α$ scales as $L^{2-α-η}$ when IR-dominated, giving $I(-2η) \sim L^{d+η}$. The decomposition $I(-2η) = I_0 \cdot I_{\mathrm{shape}}$ separates volume ($I_0 \propto N(1-m^2) \sim L^d$) from anomalous dimension ($I_{\mathrm{shape}} \sim L^η$); the volume factor accounts for the magnetization-driven per-configuration variance of $Δ$. We prove the mechanism requires $d < 2 + η$ (IR dominance), confining it to $d = 2$ for physical systems; in $d = 3$ the spectral integral is UV-dominated, explaining why density normalization is needed. An $α$-sweep for Potts $q = 4$ at $L = 32$--$256$ finds $α_{\mathrm{opt}}$ in $[-0.75, -0.5]$, consistent with $-2η_{\mathrm{Ising}}$ and inconsistent with $-2η_{q=4} = -1$; we flag this as tentative pending $L \geq 1024$ confirmation. The $\langle m^2 \cdot I(-2η)\rangle$ hyperscaling product is dominated by the correlation $r(m^2, I) \approx -0.98$ via the shared $I_0$ amplitude, so we report it as a covariance-correction analysis. Under a heuristic argument extending Divol--Polonik to inhomogeneous Poisson intensities, the bare PH kernel is flat; the effective kernel acquires $k$-dependence only at criticality. The per-configuration agreement between $Δ$ and $I(-0.5)$ is primarily a magnetization correlation: $R^2 = 0.91$ at $L = 256$ collapses to $R^2 \approx 0$ once $|M|$ is partialed out. Per-configuration evidence corroborates the $I_0$ Parseval identity but not the $|k|^{-2η}$ shape factor; the latter is established by ensemble $L$-scaling.

</details>


### [77] [Physics-informed neural networks by Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling (3GAS-PINNs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09162)
*Yousen Wang,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出梯度引导高斯自适应采样PINN（3GAS-PINN），通过混合均匀与高斯平滑概率分布自适应配置配点，提升非线性间断结构求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN在非线性问题中收敛慢、梯度不平衡，且因采用固定损失权重和均匀配点分布，难以分辨激波等局部间断结构，无法适应训练中解场复杂度的演化。

Method: 3GAS-PINN将均匀概率分布与由解的空间梯度导出的高斯平滑概率分布相结合，在保持全局约束满足的同时，将配点集中于高梯度区域，从而精确捕捉激波、孤子等间断结构。

Result: 在一维强迫Burgers方程、KdV方程与非线性Schrödinger方程三个具有陡梯度或强非线性的基准问题上评估；相较基线PINN，3GAS-PINN有效提升间断区域的物理一致性，数值模拟精度最高提升14倍。

Conclusion: 梯度引导的自适应采样可显著改善PINN在非线性间断问题中的精度与物理一致性，验证了配点分布自适应策略的有效性。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a mesh-free framework for solving partial differential equations, yet their performance in nonlinear problems is often limited by slow convergence, gradient imbalance, and insufficient resolution to capture localized intermittent structures such as shock waves[1]. These issues arise primarily from the use of fixed weights of loss and uniform collocation point distributions, which cannot adapt to the evolving complexity of the solution field during training. To address these challenges, Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling Physics-Informed Neural Networks (3GAS-PINNs) is proposed in this paper, which combines uniform probability distribution and Gaussian-smoothed probability distribution derived from the spatial gradients of solution, to maintain global constraint satisfaction as well as concentrating collocation points in regions of high gradient. Thus, intermittency structures like shock wave and solitons can be accurately captured. The method is evaluated on three benchmark nonlinear problems, including one-dimensional forced Burgers equation, Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) equation and nonlinear Schrodinger equation, all of which exhibit steep gradients or strong nonlinearity. In comparison with baseline PINNs, 3GAS-PINNs can effectively promote the physical consistency in intermittent regions. The accuracy of the numerical simulation can be improved by a factor of up to 14.

</details>


### [78] [World-Time Compute with Verified Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09163)
*James Schwoebel,Ingrida Semenec,Jenia Rousseva,Marcos Ortiz,Collin Overbay,Christopher Klaus,Anderson Edmond,Manish Bhatt,Rome Thorstenson,Jessica Tsai,Martin G. Frasch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将领域动态写成代码后，一个模板可实例化出大量可执行、可验证的世界模型，为LLM提供带精确标签的无穷轨迹。用这些轨迹做微调（world-time compute）能提升对未训练过的留出世界的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的跨领域泛化依赖大量真实标注样本，而多数领域缺乏此类数据。作者希望找到一种廉价"制造"这种监督信号的方式，即利用可写成代码的领域动态作为无限精确标签来源。

Method: 当领域动态可写成代码时，用单一模板实例化出许多世界模型——以符号状态为基础的可执行、可验证程序，每个都是精确标注轨迹的无穷来源。在这些世界中生成的轨迹上微调LLM，即world-time compute。同时设置损坏标签对照、与domain randomization对比，并在ARC-AGI网格、List Functions、CLRS等真实基准上以per-world test-time training方式验证。

Result: 增益在能力最稀缺处最大：0.5B模型+29分；最大模型提升在噪声内，符合饱和趋势。合成后校验的动态在20步rollout上精确，对分布外探针100%正确，而逐步LLM/MLP预测器误差累积并崩溃。在List Functions上跨世界形式成立：一个适配器在128个不相交世界上训练，留出世界达40%，损坏标签对照6%（+34分，CI[29,39]）。增益是饱和的规律而非定律。

Conclusion: 标签精确性（而非任务多样性）驱动增益；增益对少步推理与小/弱模型最大，在长链、感知类任务和已饱和任务上减弱，跨任务迁移在无共享技能时较弱。范围限于符号状态，像素原生领域仍属学习模型的范畴。

Abstract: LLMs generalize across a domain only after seeing many real, labeled examples, which most domains lack. We study a way to manufacture it cheaply. When a domain's dynamics can be written as code, one template instantiates into many world models: executable, verifiable programs over symbolic state, each an inexhaustible source of exactly-labeled trajectories. Fine-tuning an LLM on trajectories through many such worlds, which we call world-time compute, a training-time analogue of test-time compute, lifts generalization to held-out worlds it never trained on (synthesized world families). Gains are largest where capability is scarcest: +29 points at 0.5B; the largest model's lift is within noise, consistent with saturation. Labels can be trusted because the worlds are verified code: synthesized-then-checked dynamics are exact over 20-step rollouts and answer 10x out-of-distribution probes exactly (100%), whereas per-step LLM and MLP predictors compound error and collapse. Unlike domain randomization, each world is independently authored and verified; a corrupted-label control shows label exactness, not task variety, drives the gains. On real benchmarks (ARC-AGI grids, List Functions, CLRS) the same lever holds as per-world test-time training. On List Functions the harder cross-world form holds: one adapter trained on 128 disjoint worlds reaches 40% on held-out worlds versus 6% for a corrupted-label control (+34 points, CI [29, 39]). The gain is a saturating regularity, not a law: largest for few-step reasoning and small/weak models, fading for long chains, perception-induced tasks, and saturated tasks; cross-task transfer is weak without shared skill. Worlds are authored and served by OpenWorld, a zero-dependency framework (companion paper). Scope: symbolic state; pixel-native domains remain territory of learned models. All code, recipes, and this manuscript regenerate from one repository.

</details>


### [79] [Scaling Post-Training Ternarisation to Qwen3-8B Capability Retention, Reproduction, Lossless Packing, and Packed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09240)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对Qwen3-4B至Qwen3-8B的超低比特(1.58-bit)后训练转换流程进行端到端放大验证，量化8B模型能力损失、打包表示与直接执行表现，作为可复现的缩放基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "1.58-bit"标签本身并不说明实际部署表示与执行开销，且超低比特量化从4B放大到8B时的能力保持、序列化与推理效率缺乏端到端验证。

Method: 采用KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ自适应三值化与GPTQ式误差补偿，权重-only A16配置；外部复现门控、匹配的4B/8B能力分析、跨语料困惑度、有效比特核算、无损格点感知打包与直接打包执行。

Result: 8B三语料困惑度比1.361x（WikiText-2 1.318x、C4 1.393x、PTB 1.371x）；八个零样本任务(n=500)平均准确率64.6% vs FP16 72.4%，机会校正保留率78.5%，绝对代价7.8点；匹配4B保留69.6%，8B领先8.9点。打包检查点8.24 GiB并保持困惑度；直接打包执行15.52 tokens/s@7.35 GiB，打包GEMV仍慢于FP16 cuBLAS。

Conclusion: 模型规模提升增强了对激进后训练离散化的鲁棒性，序列化在实测工件上已解决，直接执行可行；但更广泛随机种子、校准分布与内核优化仍待解决。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models promise reductions in storage and memory traffic, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not specify the deployed representation or its execution cost. We study a scale-up of an aggressive post-training conversion pipeline from Qwen3-4B to Qwen3-8B.
  The conversion uses KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ adaptive ternarisation, and GPTQ-style error compensation in a weight-only A16 configuration. We do not claim these algorithms as new. Our contribution is the end-to-end scale-up characterisation: an external reproduction gate, matched 4B/8B capability analysis, cross-corpus perplexity, effective-bit accounting, lossless lattice-aware packing, and direct packed execution.
  The 8B model reaches a three-corpus perplexity ratio of 1.361x, with WikiText-2, C4, and PTB ratios of 1.318x, 1.393x, and 1.371x. On eight zero-shot tasks at n = 500, mean accuracy is 64.6% versus 72.4% for FP16, corresponding to 78.5% chance-corrected retention and a 7.8-point absolute cost. The matched 4B run retains 69.6%, yielding an 8.9-point 8B advantage.
  The packed checkpoint is 8.24 GiB and preserves the recorded perplexity to measurement precision. Direct packed execution reaches 15.52 tokens/s in 7.35 GiB, while a preliminary packed GEMV remains slower than FP16 cuBLAS. The result is a validated scale-up baseline: model size improves robustness to aggressive post-training discretisation, actual serialisation is solved for the measured artefact, and direct execution is feasible, while broader seeds, calibration distributions, and kernel optimisation remain open.

</details>


### [80] [Distribution-Consistent Inference for Dynamic Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09241)
*Dohyeon Kim,Bedionita Soro,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Layer-wise Distribution Alignment (LDA)，一种轻量级推理时校正方法，用于缓解MoE模型在减少专家激活数量时产生的分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数MoE模型采用固定的top-k专家选择策略，为每个token分配相同的专家预算，即使某些token用更少专家就足够。推理时的动态top-k路由可以在不重训练的情况下减少计算，但现有方法忽视了偏离训练时路由配置所导致的分布偏移，这会造成表示不匹配进而导致下游性能下降。

Method: 作者先揭示减少激活专家数量会一致性地增加SMoE输出的RMS尺度和方差，造成表示不匹配。为此提出Layer-wise Distribution Alignment (LDA)，该轻量级推理时校正方法利用逐层校准统计量，将减少路由后的表示与默认配置对齐。

Result: 在多个SMoE大语言模型、基准测试和路由策略上，LDA能够恢复因减少路由导致的分布偏移所损失的大部分性能，同时以可忽略的开销保持稀疏推理效率。

Conclusion: 减少专家激活数量引起的性能下降中包含一个可校正的分布偏移成分，通过逐层分布对齐可以有效缓解该问题，从而在保持稀疏推理效率的同时提升动态top-k路由的实用性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have emerged as a powerful paradigm for scaling model capacity while preserving efficient inference in large foundation models. However, most MoE models use a fixed top-$k$ expert selection policy, assigning the same expert budget to every token even when fewer experts may be sufficient. Inference-time dynamic top-$k$ routing can reduce computation without retraining, but existing methods often overlook the distributional shift caused by deviating from the training-time routing configuration. We show that reducing the number of activated experts consistently increases the RMS scale and variance of SMoE outputs, inducing a representation mismatch that contributes to downstream performance degradation in addition to the loss of expert capacity. To address this correctable component, we propose Layer-wise Distribution Alignment (LDA), a lightweight inference-time correction that uses layer-wise calibration statistics to align reduced-routing representations with the default configuration. Across multiple SMoE LLMs, benchmarks, and routing strategies, LDA recovers much of the performance lost induced by the distributional shift under reduced routing while preserving sparse-inference efficiency with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [81] [DiffLUT-Net: Differentiable Training of FPGA LUT Networks with Learnable Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09254)
*Jiaqi Ye,Xinrui Gong,Jingcun Wang,Olga Kondrateva,Bing Li,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffLUT-Net是一种FPGA原生神经网络，由六输入查找表（LUT）连接而成，通过可微LUT函数松弛与硬件源选择从头联合训练LUT真值表和输入端口连接，训练后离散化并直接导出为可综合Verilog。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPGA神经网络部署流程主要分为两类：一类加速乘累加（MAC）运算，另一类将预训练量化模型转换为查找表。这两类方法均未实现网络结构与LUT逻辑的从头联合学习，难以获得紧凑的FPGA原生推理实现。

Method: 提出DiffLUT-Net，一个由六输入LUT连接构成的FPGA原生网络，从零开始训练。核心方法包括：（1）使用可微LUT函数松弛，联合学习每个LUT的64个真值表条目；（2）通过硬件源选择机制学习每个LUT六个输入端口的来源连接。训练完成后对真值表和连接进行离散化，并剪枝未使用的逻辑，最终直接导出为可综合的Verilog代码。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，DiffLUT-Net取得了良好的精度-资源权衡（accuracy-resource trade-offs）。

Conclusion: 结果证明了联合学习LUT函数与稀疏连接对于实现紧凑FPGA原生推理的有效性。代码已开源于https://github.com/TUDa-HWAI/DiffLUT-Network。

Abstract: Field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) enable efficient neural-network inference, but most deployment flows either accelerate multiply-accumulate operations or convert pretrained quantized models into lookup tables (LUTs). We present DiffLUT-Net, an FPGA-native network connected by six-input LUTs that are trained from scratch. We jointly learn the 64 truth-table entries of a LUT and the source to each of its six input ports using a differentiable LUT function relaxation and hardware source selection. After training, the truth tables and connections are discretized, unused logic can be pruned, and the network is exported directly as synthesizable Verilog. Across five benchmarks, DiffLUT-Net achieves favorable accuracy-resource trade-offs. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly learning LUT functions and sparse connectivity for compact FPGA-native inference. The code is available at https://github.com/TUDa-HWAI/DiffLUT-Network.

</details>


### [82] [Accountable and uncertainty-aware evaluation of sensor-based AI under distribution shift: devices, subjects, and nearly three years underground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09257)
*Benny Platte,Rico Thomanek,Christian Roschke,Marc Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分阶段、可问责的评估协议，将已部署模型的评估视为带声明参考水平和量化不确定度的测量，通过四类累积泛化阶段（留出设备、受试者、时间）并结合分位数判定规则，在地下矿井地磁定位任务中验证了该协议的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于传感器的AI系统很少在训练条件下运行：设备、人员和记录时段都会变化，每次变化都会以随机训练-测试划分无法揭示的方式降低性能。因此需要一种能显式量化泛化不确定度、避免均值误导的评估协议。

Method: 提出分阶段、可问责的评估协议：包含四个累积泛化阶段，分别留出设备、受试者和时间；每个阶段基于重复训练的分位数与具有正确类别数的随机参考进行判定；引入"超出当前范围率"以暴露对训练期存在但部署期已消失类别的静默误导向；采用显式决策规则，将部署决策绑定到5%分位数而非均值。在无基础设施依赖的智能手机地磁定位任务、两座真实地下矿井中验证（含第二站点训练阶段复现）。

Result: 未改动模型在训练活动34个月后记录的数据、训练时未知的设备代际以及留出测量员条件下重新评估。其当前条件精确率的5%分位数在299次重复训练中为0.39，是随机水平的16.5倍；在42个可达位置类别构成上该数值波动±0.08，为数倍于重复运行之间的差异。单一配置的重复训练显示均值具有误导性：某双峰配置在均值检验中决定性通过，但其5%分位数却低于随机水平一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该协议能够揭示随机划分所掩盖的泛化失败，通过分位数和超出范围率等指标提供对部署模型性能更稳健、更可问责的评估，适用于设备、人员和时段发生变化的真实传感器AI部署场景。

Abstract: Sensor-based AI systems are rarely operated under the conditions under which they were trained: devices, personnel and recording epochs change, and each change degrades performance in ways a random train-test split cannot reveal. We propose a staged, accountable evaluation protocol that treats the evaluation of a deployed model as a measurement with declared reference levels and a quantified uncertainty. Four cumulative generalisation stages hold out devices, subjects and time. Each stage is judged on quantiles of repeated trainings against chance references with the correct class count, an out-of-present-scope rate exposes silent misdirection towards classes that are no longer present in deployment relative to training, and an explicit decision rule ties roll-out decisions not to means but to 5% quantiles. We demonstrate the protocol on infrastructure-free geomagnetic localisation with smartphone-based recurrent classifiers in two real underground mines, including a replication of the scheme's training stages at the second site. Unchanged models are re-evaluated on data recorded 34 months after the training campaign, on a device generation unknown at training time and with a held-out surveyor. The 5% quantile of their present-conditioned precision there is 0.39 over 299 repeated trainings, 16.5 times the chance level; across the composition of the 42 reachable location classes the figure varies by +/-0.08, several times the spread between repeated runs. Repeated trainings of a single configuration show why means mislead: a bimodal configuration passes a mean-based test decisively while its 5% quantile lies more than an order of magnitude below chance.

</details>


### [83] [Literati: Towards Anytime Optimal Shape Generalized Trees via AO*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09299)
*Nakul Upadhya,Eldan Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Literati，首个面向 Shape Generalized Trees（SGTs）的最优归纳算法：将树结构与形状函数复杂度的联合优化建模为 AND/OR 图，并用带两种增强的 AO* 求解，在 24 个真实数据集上取得优于 SOTA 树模型的训练与测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树可解释且擅长表格数据，但贪婪自顶向下归纳易产生次优且过深的结构；最优决策树方法虽用全局优化解决该问题，却局限于轴对齐阈值分裂，节点表达力有限，往往需要深层复杂树才能刻画非线性特征效应。SGT 将阈值分裂推广为可学习的单变量形状函数，能在更紧凑的树中提升表达力，但现有 SGT 归纳均为贪婪算法，缺乏最优性保证。

Method: 提出 Literati：将最优 SGT 归纳问题建模为一种新颖的 AND/OR 图，在该图中联合优化树结构与形状函数复杂度；针对该图设计基于 AO* 的求解算法，并引入两项在保持最优性的同时改善 anytime 性能的增强——用于 OR 节点选择的次级启发式，以及用于 AND 节点探索的轮询（round-robin）策略。

Result: 在 24 个真实世界数据集上，Literati 的训练精度与测试精度均高于当前最先进的树方法。

Conclusion: Literati 首次为 SGT 归纳提供了最优性保证，突破了现有 SGT 贪婪算法无最优性保证的限制，并通过 AND/OR 图建模与增强 AO* 搜索，在精度上超越 SOTA 树方法。

Abstract: Decision trees are prized for their interpretability and strong performance on tabular data, but popular greedy top-down induction algorithms can yield suboptimal and unnecessarily complex structures. Optimal decision tree methods address this through global optimization, yet remain restricted to axis-aligned threshold splits, which limit the expressivity of each node and often force deep, complex trees to capture non-linear feature effects. Shape Generalized Trees (SGTs) generalize threshold splits to learnable univariate shape functions, improving expressivity and enabling more compact trees. However, existing SGT induction algorithms are greedy and offer no optimality guarantees. In this work, we introduce Literati, the first algorithm for optimal SGT induction. We propose a novel AND/OR graph formulation of the problem that jointly optimizes tree structure and shape function complexity. To solve this AND/OR graph, we develop an AO*-based algorithm with two enhancements that improve anytime performance while preserving optimality: a secondary heuristic for OR-node selection and a round-robin policy for AND-node exploration. Across 24 real-world datasets, Literati achieves higher training and test accuracy than state-of-the-art tree approaches.

</details>


### [84] [Explaining f-Divergence-Based Regularization via Local Curvature and Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09367)
*Nour Jamoussi,Marios Kountouris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从二阶展开出发，揭示散度正则化与SAM在参数空间扰动下局部一致，并推广到输入空间扰动，用α-skew JSD实验验证曲率惩罚与泛化性能的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散度正则化与Sharpness-Aware Minimization（SAM）都旨在通过扰动鲁棒性提升深度学习的泛化能力，但二者之间的关系长期缺乏系统研究。作者希望从统一的几何视角厘清二者的联系，并将其扩展到输入空间扰动，以构建更一般的扰动框架。

Method: 基于f-散度的经典二阶展开，分析散度正则化与SAM在参数空间扰动下的局部一致性：散度正则化产生Fisher加权的二次型，SAM通过主Hessian特征值惩罚锐度；对指数族输出的负对数似然目标，Fisher矩阵与Gauss-Newton矩阵一致，使对应关系尤为清晰。进一步将局部几何视角扩展到输入空间扰动，散度正则化通过输入变换定义，诱导输入空间上的拉回二次型。以非对称α-skew Jensen-Shannon散度（JSD）族作为受控实验平台，其局部曲率系数为α(1-α)，在对称点α=1/2处最大。

Result: 输入扰动区制下的损失景观可视化显示，更强的诱导曲率惩罚与更平坦的局部极小值相关；在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，准确率和负对数似然在该最大曲率惩罚区制附近一致达到最优。

Conclusion: 散度正则化与SAM在局部几何上具有一致性，均表现为曲率敏感的惩罚项；该统一视角可推广至输入空间扰动，提供比标准SAM更一般的扰动框架。α-skew JSD实验验证了理论分析，表明曲率惩罚强度与泛化性能密切相关。

Abstract: Divergence-based regularization and Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) are two prominent approaches for improving generalization in deep learning, both motivated by robustness to perturbations. However, their relationship has remained largely unexplored. Building on classical second-order expansions of $f$-divergences, we show that the two methods are locally consistent under parameter-space perturbations: both induce curvature-sensitive penalties, with divergence regularization yielding a Fisher-weighted quadratic form and SAM penalizing sharpness through the dominant Hessian eigenvalue. For negative log-likelihood objectives with exponential-family output distributions, this correspondence becomes especially transparent, since the Fisher and Gauss-Newton matrices coincide. We further show that the same local geometric perspective extends to input-space perturbations, where divergence-based regularization is defined through transformations of the input. In this setting, the regularizer induces a pullback quadratic form on the input space, providing a more general perturbation framework than standard SAM while preserving the same local sensitivity interpretation. To validate the analysis empirically, we use the asymmetric $α$-skew Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD) family as a controlled testbed. Its local curvature coefficient scales as $α(1-α)$ and is maximized at the symmetric point $α=\tfrac12$, which recovers the standard JSD. Loss-landscape visualizations in the input-perturbation regime show that stronger induced curvature penalization is associated with flatter local minima. Experiments on four benchmark datasets further demonstrate that both accuracy and negative log-likelihood are consistently best near this regime of maximal curvature penalization.

</details>


### [85] [Constraint-Aware Discrete Black-Box Optimization Using Tensor Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09370)
*Keisuke Onoue,Ryosuke Kojima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于张量分解的代理模型，将可行性信息直接嵌入离散黑箱优化，通过约束多项式优化与T-范数可微惩罚来训练，提升了样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散黑盒优化中，SMBO依赖代理模型提升样本效率。但实际问题的可行输入常由预先已知的逻辑约束决定，而现有代理建模难以在离散输入空间中刻画这些符号可行性规则。

Method: 基于张量分解构建代理模型，既能刻画离散搜索空间结构，又能直接整合可行性信息。将代理模型训练形式化为约束多项式优化问题，并利用由T-范数导出的可微惩罚项求解其松弛形式。

Result: 在合成与真实基准（含压力容器设计任务）上，该方法通过引导搜索远离不可行区域，有效提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 将可行性逻辑约束直接融入张量分解代理模型，是改善离散黑盒优化样本效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Discrete black-box optimization is often addressed using approaches such as Sequential Model-Based Optimization (SMBO), which aims to improve sample efficiency by fitting surrogate models that approximate a costly objective function over a discrete search space. In many real-world problems, the set of feasible inputs is often given by logical constraints known in advance. However, existing surrogate modeling techniques generally fail to capture the symbolic rules governing feasibility in discrete input spaces. In this paper, we propose a surrogate modeling approach based on tensor decomposition that captures the structure of discrete search spaces while directly integrating feasibility information. To implement this approach, we formulate surrogate model training as a constrained polynomial optimization problem and solve a relaxed formulation using a differentiable penalty term derived from T-norms. Our experiments on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks, including a pressure vessel design task, demonstrate that the proposed method improves sample efficiency by effectively guiding the search away from infeasible regions.

</details>


### [86] [XAI-Refine: An Automated Explanation-Knowledge Loop for Brain-Age Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09388)
*Yang Qiao,Junjie Wu,Deqiang Qiu,James J. Lah,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 XAI-Refine，一个面向静息态功能连接脑龄预测的自动化“解释-知识”闭环框架，通过将后验解释转化为可验证的神经生物学知识约束，实现证据引导的模型精炼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑龄预测模型通常仅以预测精度评价，但高精度并不能保证模型依赖可复现或具神经生物学支撑的机制。已有后验解释方法虽能揭示机制，但现有流程多止步于诊断，或在模型分析前就要求指定修正目标，缺乏自动化、证据驱动的精炼路径。

Method: XAI-Refine 在每次迭代中：1）将多次重复训练的后验分析结果整合为可靠的结构化模型解释；2）把每条可靠解释转为中性的神经生物学问题，检索并核验相关文献；3）将核验后的证据编译到同一类型化解释空间，形成可采纳集；4）以当前模型解释在可采纳集上的最小投影作为精炼目标，并将其翻译为可微约束，同时保留原模型变量、测量算子与适用域；5）仅当多种子验证确认解释朝目标移动、预测性能处于预设护栏内、非目标解释漂移有界时，才采纳候选更新。

Result: 在基于功能连接的脑龄预测实验中，从预测性能、解释可靠性、文献一致性以及目标特异性模型修正四个方面进行了评估，展示了从后验分析到证据引导模型精炼的结构化路径。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将可靠后验解释与经核验的神经生物学文献证据连接成闭环，可实现有约束、可验证的模型修正，为脑龄预测模型提供从“预测准确”走向“机制可信”的自动化精炼范式。

Abstract: Brain-age prediction models are commonly evaluated by predictive accuracy, yet accurate predictions alone do not establish that a model relies on reproducible or neurobiologically supported mechanisms. Post-hoc explanation methods can expose these mechanisms, but existing workflows typically stop at diagnosis or require correction targets to be specified before model analysis. We propose XAI-Refine, an automated explanation-knowledge loop for brain-age prediction from resting-state functional connectivity. At each iteration, XAI-Refine consolidates complementary post-hoc analyses across repeated training runs into reliable, structured model explanations. It converts each reliable explanation into a neutral neurobiological question, retrieves and verifies relevant literature, and compiles the verified evidence into an admissible set in the same typed explanation space. The target for refinement is defined as the minimal projection of the current model explanation onto the admissible set induced by applicable verified knowledge. This revised explanation is then translated into a differentiable constraint while preserving the originating model variable, measurement operator, and applicable scope. Candidate updates are promoted only when multi-seed validation confirms target-directed explanatory movement, predictive performance remains within a prespecified guardrail, and non-target explanatory drift remains bounded. Experiments on functional-connectivity-based brain-age prediction evaluate predictive performance, explanation reliability, literature alignment, and target-specific model revision, illustrating a structured route from post-hoc analysis to evidence-guided model refinement.

</details>


### [87] [Applying foundation model embeddings towards urban livability evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09429)
*Ayush Khot,Wen Zhou,Shaowen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种系统框架，用于识别基础模型（AlphaEarth、AnySat、TerraMind）地理空间嵌入中编码的物理特征，并利用这些嵌入提升数据稀缺地区城市宜居性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据稀缺地区的社会经济指标难以准确测量，制约了政策干预与资源分配；而高分辨率地理空间数据广泛可得，且蕴含多种宜居性统计信息，因此需要挖掘其预测潜力。

Method: 分析不同类型地理空间数据对城市宜居性预测的影响，研究基础模型嵌入中编码的物理特征，构建识别最具预测力地理空间指标的系统框架，并利用基础模型嵌入处理复杂空间依赖以增强预测。

Result: 方法使研究者能够针对具体应用优先选择信息量最大的特征，并证明基础模型嵌入可有效提升宜居性结果的预测性能。

Conclusion: 本工作提供了一套从卫星影像中提取可操作信息的原则性方法，兼顾复杂空间依赖，可应用于观测数据有限地区的城市宜居性预测。

Abstract: While accurate measurement of socioeconomic indicators remains challenging in data-scarce regions, which limits policy interventions and resource allocation, high-resolution geospatial data is widely available and can contain information on various livability statistics. We investigate which physical features are encoded within foundation model embeddings, such as AlphaEarth, AnySat, and TerraMind, and provide a systematic framework for identifying the most predictive geospatial indicators. By analyzing how different types of geospatial data influence urban livability predictions, our approach enables researchers to prioritize the most informative features for their specific applications. Additionally, we demonstrate how to leverage foundation model embeddings to enhance prediction performance for these outcomes. This work contributes a principled methodology for extracting actionable information from satellite imagery while accounting for complex spatial dependencies, with applications in predicting urban livability in regions with limited observation data.

</details>


### [88] [SCCM : Stream Cruise Control Method for Automated Drift Detection and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09432)
*Mohammad Abu-Shaira,Weishi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出流巡航控制方法SCCM，一个用于在线回归中概念漂移检测与自适应的综合框架，集早期预警漂移检测、漂移幅度量化、KPI窗口阈值化误报抑制、动态超参数调优与模型重校准于一体，并在18个合成数据集和8个真实数据集上取得优于8种基线方法的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集的分布会不断演化（概念漂移），忽略漂移会降低预测性能；同时固定超参数也进一步限制了模型在变化条件下的适应性。现有方法多为纯反应式，仅在观察到性能下降后才启动自适应，难以满足实时适应性需求。

Method: 提出SCCM框架，采用内存式设计实现实时自适应。其机制包括：早期响应、更新前的漂移检测、漂移幅度量化、基于KPI窗口的阈值化以缓解局部误报、动态超参数调优以及模型重校准。SCCM使用动态阈值且对数据分布保持无关性，支持跨不同数据流的KPI式监控（含高维与大规模场景），并集成到四种在线回归模型中。

Result: 在18个合成数据集（涵盖突变、增量、交替渐变漂移）和8个真实数据集上，以R2与MSE为评价指标，与8种检测器-自适应基线对比，SCCM在所评估的在线回归场景中表现出更好的预测性能与有效的漂移处理能力。

Conclusion: SCCM通过早期检测、动态阈值与在线超参数调整等综合机制，有效应对在线回归中的概念漂移，具有分布无关性与实时适应性，适用于高维、大规模等多样数据流场景，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Real-world datasets often exhibit evolving distributions, known as concept drift. Ignoring drift degrades predictive performance, while reliance on fixed hyperparameters further limits model adaptability under changing conditions. Adaptive learning addresses this challenge by continuously updating models online, allowing them to incrementally adjust and remain effective as data distributions evolve. This paper presents the Stream Cruise Control Method (SCCM), a comprehensive framework for drift detection and adaptation in online regression. SCCM enables automated adaptation through early-response, pre-update drift detection, drift magnitude quantification, KPI-window-based thresholding for local false-alarm mitigation, dynamic hyperparameter tuning, and model recalibration. SCCM also adopts an in-memory design for real-time adaptability, unlike purely reactive methods that typically activate adaptation only after performance degradation is observed. By using dynamic thresholding and remaining agnostic to data distributions, SCCM supports KPI-based monitoring across varying data streams, including high-dimensional and large-scale settings. SCCM is integrated with four online regression models and evaluated on 18 synthetic datasets covering abrupt, incremental, and alternating gradual drift, together with eight real-world datasets. The evaluation uses both R2 and MSE and compares against eight detector--adaptation baselines. Results show improved predictive performance and effective drift handling across the evaluated online regression settings.

</details>


### [89] [Efficient Leakage-Free Neural Architecture Search under Leave-One-Subject-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09433)
*Heinke Hihn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无泄漏、基于分块的NAS方法，跨被试共享搜索过程，在BioVid疼痛数据集上以更少参数取得更高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LOSO评估能估计被试间分类的泛化性能，但完全嵌套实现需N次独立架构搜索，在近似线性训练成本假设下复杂度达O(N^2)，NAS计算代价过高。

Method: 采用无泄漏、基于分块（block-based）的策略，使多个被试的NAS运行共享同一搜索过程，避免为每个被试单独搜索。

Result: 在BioVid Heat Pain数据集上，平均准确率由82.79%提升至83.39%，参数量最多减少99.2%。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不引入数据泄漏的前提下显著降低了LOSO场景下NAS的计算开销，同时提升了分类精度并大幅压缩模型规模。

Abstract: Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) evaluation estimates generalisation performance for subject-based classification but makes Neural Architecture Search (NAS) computationally expensive because a fully nested implementation requires N independent architecture searches and, assuming approximately linear training cost, scales as O(N^2). We propose a leakage-free, block-based approach that shares NAS runs across subjects. On the BioVid Heat Pain dataset, our approach increased the mean accuracy from 82.79% to 83.39% while reducing the number of parameters by up to 99.2%.

</details>


### [90] [Tensor-Train Weak SINDy: Identifying High-Dimensional Nonlinear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09434)
*Will Houser,Vanja Dukic,David M. Bortz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TT-WSINDy方法，将MANDy与WSINDy结合并在张量列（TT）格式下实现所需计算，从而在高维场景下高效进行弱形式动力系统的数据驱动发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱形式方法虽在动力系统数据驱动发现中取得显著进展，但在高维设置下计算与内存开销过高，难以扩展到指数增长的候选函数空间。

Method: 融合多维非线性动力学近似（MANDy）与弱稀疏非线性动力学辨识（WSINDy），并将弱形式变换、回归与稀疏化等核心计算全部以张量列（TT）格式实现。

Result: 该方法能够搜索指数增长的候选函数空间，在其中完成弱形式变换、回归和稀疏化，且不遭受维数灾难影响。

Conclusion: TT-WSINDy为高维动力系统的弱形式稀疏辨识提供了可扩展的计算框架，有效缓解了计算与内存瓶颈。

Abstract: In recent years, weak-form methods have made significant advances in data-driven discovery of dynamical systems. However, in high-dimensional settings, current techniques can prove expensive in both computation and memory. In this work, we introduce TT-WSINDy, which combines techniques of the Multidimensional Approximation of Nonlinear Dynamics (MANDy) and Weak Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (WSINDy) methods, implementing requisite computations in the tensor-train (TT) format. We demonstrate that this method is able to search an exponentially-growing space of candidate functions -- performing weak-form transformation, regression, and sparsification -- without suffering from the curse of dimensionality.

</details>


### [91] [Uncertainty-Aware Sea-Ice Type Mapping with Multiple Ice Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09451)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Andrew P. Barrett,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文量化海冰发展阶段（SoD）制图中的两类不确定性：多标注者标签不确定性与模型不确定性，并评估二者关系及不同监督方式的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务冰情图由分析师依据卫星观测人工判读，不同冰情服务机构对同一冰况可能给出不同SoD标签，此类多标注者分歧的标签不确定性常被合并为单一确定目标而丢弃；同时学习模型本身也存在不确定性。两者关系尚缺乏系统量化。

Method: 以独立冰情服务机构的业务冰情图作为参考标注，分别量化标注不确定性（机构间分歧）与模型不确定性（学习预测模型），并检验模型不确定性是否在冰情机构分歧更大处更高；对比单标注硬监督与融合多标注者信息的软监督，并评估多种不确定性估计方法（含Monte Carlo dropout）的置信度校准。

Result: 融合多标注者信息的监督可改善模型不确定性与标注分歧的对应关系，软监督取得最高总体相关性0.256；在冰缘附近关系显著增强，0–10 km内相关性达0.704；Monte Carlo dropout提供最佳校准置信度，期望校准误差为0.050。

Conclusion: 多标注者标签不确定性与模型不确定性存在可量化的关联，且在冰缘区域尤为紧密；采用软监督并配合MC dropout可更真实地反映标注分歧并给出良好校准的不确定性估计，为业务化SoD制图的不确定性建模提供依据。

Abstract: Sea-ice stage of development (SoD) describes the age and associated thickness of sea ice and provides important information for navigation, and operational ice monitoring. SoD labels are obtained from operational ice charts, where trained analysts interpret satellite observations and assign standardized stage codes to regions with similar ice conditions. These codes often represent ranges of compatible ice thicknesses rather than exact physical values. Deep-learning methods can automate SoD mapping and commonly adopt operational ice charts as reference labels for training. These annotations are not exact, however; this is because chart interpretation relies on analyst judgement and on the observations available at the time, so different ice services may assign different SoD labels to the same conditions. We term this variation across independently produced expert annotations multi-annotator label uncertainty; collapsing the annotations into a single deterministic target discards this variation. A second source of uncertainty originates in the learned model itself. In this paper, we quantify both sources: annotation uncertainty from disagreement among independent ice-service charts and model uncertainty from the learned predictive models. We then evaluate their relationship by testing whether model uncertainty is higher where ice services disagree. We observe that supervision incorporating information from multiple annotators can improve this correspondence, with soft supervision achieving the highest overall correlation of 0.256.
